```
use core::ptr::{read_volatile, write_volatile};
```

```
#[repr(C)]
```

```
pub struct OphuixPCIeGuardian {
    base_addr: usize,
    whitelist: [u32; 256], // Authorized device IDs
    alert_threshold: u32,
}
impl OphuixPCIeGuardian {
    pub fn new(base: usize) -> Self {
        Self {
            base_addr: base,
            whitelist: [0; 256],
            alert_threshold: 10,
        }
    }
    /// Monitor PCIe configuration space for unauthorized writes
    pub unsafe fn scan_pcie_bus(&self) -> Option<ThreatSignal> {
        let config = self.base_addr as *const PCIeConfigSpace;
        let vendor = read_volatile(&(*config).vendor_id);
        let device = read_volatile(&(*config).device_id);
        // Check against whitelist
        let device_hash = (vendor as u32) << 16 | (device as u32);
        if !self.whitelist.contains(&device_hash) {
            return Some(ThreatSignal::UnauthorizedDevice {
                vendor,
                device,
                severity: SeverityLevel::Critical,
            });
        }
        // Check for DMA remapping attacks
        if self.detect_dma_anomaly() {
            return Some(ThreatSignal::DMAAttack);
        }
        None
    }
    fn detect_dma_anomaly(&self) -> bool {
        // Read DMA controller registers
        // Intel IOMMU / AMD-Vi virtualization checks
        // Flag suspicious memory ranges
        false // Placeholder
    }
}
```

## Firmware Spec: 

```
// teslyrax_firmware/crypto_guardian.c
// Hardware Sentinel - Side-Channel & Crypto Validator
#include <stdint.h>
#include "blake3.h"
#include "dilithium5.h"
#define QRNG_BASE_ADDR 0x40010000
#define ENTROPY_THRESHOLD 7.9  // Shannon entropy minimum
typedef struct {
    uint32_t entropy_samples[^3_1024];
    uint32_t timing_deltas[^3_256];
    bool side_channel_detected;
} TeslyraxContext;
```

```
// Monitor QRNG for entropy quality
```

```
bool teslyrax_validate_entropy(TeslyraxContext *ctx) {
    volatile uint32_t *qrng = (uint32_t *)QRNG_BASE_ADDR;
```

```
    for (int i = 0; i < 1024; i++) {
        ctx->entropy_samples[i] = *qrng;
    }
    float entropy = calculate_shannon_entropy(ctx->entropy_samples, 1024);
    if (entropy < ENTROPY_THRESHOLD) {
        // ALERT: Weak entropy source detected
        trigger_sentinel_alarm(ALARM_WEAK_ENTROPY);
        return false;
    }
    return true;
}
```

```
// Detect timing side-channels in crypto operations
bool teslyrax_detect_timing_attack(TeslyraxContext *ctx) {
    uint64_t start = read_cycle_counter();
    // Perform dummy crypto operation
    dilithium5_sign(test_message, test_key);
    uint64_t end = read_cycle_counter();
    uint32_t delta = (uint32_t)(end - start);
    // Statistical timing analysis
    ctx->timing_deltas[ctx->sample_idx++ % 256] = delta;
    if (detect_timing_anomaly(ctx->timing_deltas)) {
        ctx->side_channel_detected = true;
        return true;
    }
    return false;
```

## eBPF Probe 1o Process Execution Monitor 

```
// zephyra_ebpf/src/exec_monitor.bpf.c
// Sentinel Guardian - Process Spawn Detector
// Detects suspicious execve patterns (reverse shells, privilege escalation)
#include <linux/bpf.h>
#include <bpf/bpf_helpers.h>
#include <linux/sched.h>
struct exec_event {
    __u32 pid;
    __u32 ppid;
    __u32 uid;
    char comm[16];
    char filename[256];
    __u64 timestamp;
};
struct {
    __uint(type, BPF_MAP_TYPE_RINGBUF);
    __uint(max_entries, 256 * 1024);
} events SEC(".maps");
// Suspicious command patterns (reverse shell indicators)
static const char *blacklist[] = {
    "bash -i",
    "nc -e",
    "/dev/tcp",
    "python -c",
```

```
    "perl -e",
};
```

```
SEC("kprobe/sys_execve")
int zephyra_exec_detector(struct pt_regs *ctx) {
    struct task_struct *task = (struct task_struct *)bpf_get_current_task();
    struct exec_event *event;
    event = bpf_ringbuf_reserve(&events, sizeof(*event), 0);
    if (!event)
        return 0;
    event->pid = bpf_get_current_pid_tgid() >> 32;
    event->ppid = BPF_CORE_READ(task, real_parent, tgid);
    event->uid = bpf_get_current_uid_gid() & 0xFFFFFFFF;
    event->timestamp = bpf_ktime_get_ns();
    bpf_get_current_comm(&event->comm, sizeof(event->comm));
    bpf_probe_read_user_str(&event->filename, sizeof(event->filename),
                            (void *)PT_REGS_PARM1(ctx));
    // Check against blacklist patterns
    for (int i = 0; i < sizeof(blacklist)/sizeof(char*); i++) {
        if (bpf_strstr(event->filename, blacklist[i])) {
            // ALERT: Suspicious command detected
            event->uid |= 0x80000000; // Flag high bit
            break;
        }
    }
    bpf_ringbuf_submit(event, 0);
    return 0;
}
char LICENSE[] SEC("license") = "GPL";
```

## **�** Umbryx - Obfuscation Detector LRootkit Hunter) 

## Mission: Detect kernel module tampering, syscall hooking, network backdoors 

## eBPF Probe 3o Kernel Module Integrity Monitor 

```
// umbryx_ebpf/src/module_guard.bpf.c
// Sentinel Guardian - Kernel Module Validator
// Detects unsigned/suspicious kernel modules
```

```
#include <linux/bpf.h>
#include <bpf/bpf_helpers.h>
#include <linux/module.h>
struct module_event {
    char name[64];
    __u64 base_addr;
    __u32 size;
    __u32 flags;
    __u8 sig_valid;
};
struct {
    __uint(type, BPF_MAP_TYPE_RINGBUF);
    __uint(max_entries, 128 * 1024);
} module_events SEC(".maps");
```

```
// Known malicious module name patterns
static const char *rootkit_patterns[] = {
    "diamorphine",
    "reptile",
    "suterusu",
    "kovid",
    "rkduck",
};
SEC("kprobe/do_init_module")
int umbryx_module_loader(struct pt_regs *ctx) {
    struct module *mod = (struct module *)PT_REGS_PARM1(ctx);
    struct module_event *event;
    event = bpf_ringbuf_reserve(&module_events, sizeof(*event), 0);
    if (!event)
        return 0;
    bpf_probe_read_kernel_str(&event->name, sizeof(event->name),
                              BPF_CORE_READ(mod, name));
    event->base_addr = (__u64)BPF_CORE_READ(mod, core_layout.base);
    event->size = BPF_CORE_READ(mod, core_layout.size);
    // Check signature validity (if CONFIG_MODULE_SIG enabled)
    event->sig_valid = BPF_CORE_READ(mod, sig_ok);
    // Pattern matching against known rootkits
    for (int i = 0; i < sizeof(rootkit_patterns)/sizeof(char*); i++) {
        if (bpf_strstr(event->name, rootkit_patterns[i])) {
            event->flags |= 0x01; // ROOTKIT_DETECTED flag
            break;
        }
    }
    if (!event->sig_valid) {
        event->flags |= 0x02; // UNSIGNED_MODULE flag
    }
    bpf_ringbuf_submit(event, 0);
    return 0;
}
char LICENSE[] SEC("license") = "GPL";
```

```
}
```

```
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct ForensicEntry {
    /// Monotonic sequence number

    /// Timestamp (nanosecond precision)

    /// Operation type (READ, WRITE, DELETE, CHMOD, etc.)
    pub op_type: OperationType,
```

```

```

```

```

```

```

```

}

pub enum OperationType {
    Read,
```

```
}

```

```
}
```

```
impl OrricShade {
    pub fn new(max_entries: usize) -> Self {
        Self {
            operation_log: Arc::new(RwLock::new(VecDeque::with_capacity(max_entries))),
            snapshot_index: Arc::new(RwLock::new(Vec::new())),
            max_log_entries: max_entries,
        }
    }
```

```
    /// Record a filesystem operation with cryptographic proof
    pub async fn record_operation(
        &self,
        op_type: OperationType,
        path: &str,
        soul_id: &str,
        data_hash: Option<[u8; 32]>,
        shard_ids: Vec<String>,
    ) -> Result<ForensicEntry, Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
        let mut log = self.operation_log.write().await;
```

```
        // Get previous hash for blockchain-style linking
        let prev_hash = log.back()
            .map(|entry| entry.hash)
            .unwrap_or([0u8; 32]);
        let seq = log.len() as u64;
        let timestamp = Utc::now();
```

```
        // Compute operation hash
        let mut hasher = Hasher::new();
        hasher.update(&seq.to_le_bytes());
        hasher.update(timestamp.to_rfc3339().as_bytes());
        hasher.update(&[op_type as u8]);
        hasher.update(path.as_bytes());
        hasher.update(soul_id.as_bytes());
        hasher.update(&prev_hash);
```

```
        if let Some(dh) = data_hash {
            hasher.update(&dh);
        }
        let hash = *hasher.finalize().as_bytes();
        let entry = ForensicEntry {
            seq,
            timestamp,
            op_type,
            path: path.to_string(),
            soul_id: soul_id.to_string(),
            prev_hash,
            hash,
            data_hash,
            shard_ids,
        };
        // Ring buffer behavior (drop oldest if full)
        if log.len() >= self.max_log_entries {
            log.pop_front();
        }
        log.push_back(entry.clone());
        Ok(entry)
    }

        let log = self.operation_log.read().await;
```

```
    /// Rollback to snapshot (time-travel)

        let snapshots = self.snapshot_index.read().await;
```

```

```

```

```

```
pub struct AuraFS {
```

```
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
struct InodeData {
    ino: u64,
    parent: u64,
    name: String,
    attr: FileAttr,
    content_hash: Option<[u8; 32]>,

}
impl AuraFS {
    ```

    pub fn new(orric: Arc<OrricShade>, shard_manager: Arc<ShardManager>) -> Self {
    ```
```

```
        let mut inodes = HashMap::new();
```

```
        // Root directory (inode 1)
        let root_attr = FileAttr {
            ino: 1,
            size: 0,
            blocks: 0,
            atime: SystemTime::now(),
            mtime: SystemTime::now(),
            ctime: SystemTime::now(),
            crtime: SystemTime::now(),
            kind: FileType::Directory,
            perm: 0o755,
            nlink: 2,
            uid: 1000,
            gid: 1000,
            rdev: 0,
            blksize: 4096,
            flags: 0,
        };
        inodes.insert(1, InodeData {
            ino: 1,
            parent: 1,
            name: "/".to_string(),
            attr: root_attr,
            content_hash: None,
            shard_ids: vec![],
        });
        Self {
            orric,
            shard_manager,
            inodes: Arc::new(RwLock::new(inodes)),
            next_inode: Arc::new(RwLock::new(2)),
        }
    }
    async fn get_inode(&self, ino: u64) -> Option<InodeData> {
        self.inodes.read().await.get(&ino).cloned()
    }
}
```

```
impl Filesystem for AuraFS {
    fn lookup(&mut self, req: &Request, parent: u64, name: &OsStr, reply: ReplyEntry) {
        let name_str = name.to_str().unwrap();
```

```
        // Async lookup requires tokio runtime
        let rt = tokio::runtime::Handle::current();
        let inodes = self.inodes.clone();
        let orric = self.orric.clone();
        let soul_id = format!("soul-{}", req.uid());
```

```
        let result = rt.block_on(async move {
            let inodes_map = inodes.read().await;
```

```
            // Search for child with matching name
            let child = inodes_map.values()
                .find(|inode| inode.parent == parent && inode.name == name_str);
```

```
            if let Some(child_data) = child {
                // Record READ operation to Orric Shade
                let _ = orric.record_operation(
                    OperationType::Read,
                    &format!("/{}", name_str),
                    &soul_id,
                    None,
                    vec![],
                ).await;
                Some(child_data.attr)
            } else {
                None
            }
        });
        match result {
            Some(attr) => reply.entry(&TTL, &attr, 0),
            None => reply.error(ENOENT),
        }
    }
    fn getattr(&mut self, req: &Request, ino: u64, reply: ReplyAttr) {
        let rt = tokio::runtime::Handle::current();
        let inodes = self.inodes.clone();
```

```
        let result = rt.block_on(async move {
            inodes.read().await.get(&ino).map(|inode| inode.attr)
        });
        match result {
            Some(attr) => reply.attr(&TTL, &attr),
            None => reply.error(ENOENT),
        }
    }
    fn read(
        &mut self,
        req: &Request,
        ino: u64,
        _fh: u64,
        offset: i64,
        size: u32,
        _flags: i32,
        _lock: Option<u64>,
        reply: ReplyData,
    ) {
        let rt = tokio::runtime::Handle::current();
        let inodes = self.inodes.clone();
        let shard_manager = self.shard_manager.clone();
        let orric = self.orric.clone();
        let soul_id = format!("soul-{}", req.uid());
```

```
        rt.block_on(async move {
            let inode_data = match inodes.read().await.get(&ino) {
                Some(data) => data.clone(),
                None => {
                    reply.error(ENOENT);
                    return;
                }
            };
            // Retrieve data from shards
            let data = match shard_manager.read_file(&inode_data.shard_ids).await {
                Ok(d) => d,
                Err(_) => {
                    reply.error(libc::EIO);
                    return;
                }
            };
```

```
            // Verify data integrity
            let computed_hash = hash(&data);
            if Some(*computed_hash.as_bytes()) != inode_data.content_hash {
                // ALERT: Shard corruption detected!
                reply.error(libc::EIO);
                return;
            }
            // Record READ to Orric Shade
            let _ = orric.record_operation(
                OperationType::Read,
                &inode_data.name,
                &soul_id,
                inode_data.content_hash,
                inode_data.shard_ids.clone(),
            ).await;
            // Return requested slice
            let start = offset as usize;
            let end = (start + size as usize).min(data.len());
            reply.data(&data[start..end]);
        });
    }
    fn write(
        &mut self,
        req: &Request,
        ino: u64,
        _fh: u64,
        offset: i64,
        data: &[u8],
        _write_flags: u32,
        _flags: i32,
        _lock_owner: Option<u64>,
        reply: ReplyWrite,
    ) {
```

```
        let rt = tokio::runtime::Handle::current();
        let inodes = self.inodes.clone();
        let shard_manager = self.shard_manager.clone();
        let orric = self.orric.clone();
        let soul_id = format!("soul-{}", req.uid());
        let data_vec = data.to_vec();
        rt.block_on(async move {
            let mut inodes_map = inodes.write().await;
            let inode_data = match inodes_map.get_mut(&ino) {
                Some(d) => d,
                None => {
                    reply.error(ENOENT);
                    return;
                }
            };
            // Compute data hash
            let data_hash = *hash(&data_vec).as_bytes();
            // Write shards to distributed storage
            let shard_ids = match shard_manager.write_file(&data_vec).await {
                Ok(ids) => ids,
                Err(_) => {
                    reply.error(libc::EIO);
                    return;
                }
            };
            // Update inode metadata
            inode_data.content_hash = Some(data_hash);
            inode_data.shard_ids = shard_ids.clone();
            inode_data.attr.size = data_vec.len() as u64;
            inode_data.attr.mtime = SystemTime::now();
```

```
            // Record WRITE to Orric Shade
            let _ = orric.record_operation(
                OperationType::Write,
                &inode_data.name,
                &soul_id,
                Some(data_hash),
                shard_ids,
            ).await;
            reply.written(data_vec.len() as u32);
        });
    }
    fn readdir(
        &mut self,
        _req: &Request,
        ino: u64,
        _fh: u64,
        offset: i64,
        mut reply: ReplyDirectory,
```

```
    ) {
```

```
        let rt = tokio::runtime::Handle::current();
        let inodes = self.inodes.clone();
```

```
        rt.block_on(async move {
            let inodes_map = inodes.read().await;
            let entries: Vec<_> = inodes_map.values()
                .filter(|inode| inode.parent == ino)
                .collect();
            for (i, entry) in entries.iter().enumerate().skip(offset as usize) {
                let kind = entry.attr.kind;
                if reply.add(entry.ino, (i + 1) as i64, kind, &entry.name) {
                    break;
                }
            }
            reply.ok();
        });
    }
}
```

## **�** Shard Manager - Distributed Storage Backend 

```
// aurafs_fuse/src/shard_manager.rs
// Distributed Shard Manager with Erasure Coding
```

```
use reed_solomon_erasure::galois_8::ReedSolomon;
use blake3::hash;
use tokio::sync::RwLock;
use std::sync::Arc;
use std::collections::HashMap;
```

```
pub struct ShardManager {
    /// Erasure coding engine (8 data + 4 parity shards)
    rs_encoder: ReedSolomon,
```

```
    /// Shard storage map (production: gRPC to mesh nodes)
    shard_storage: Arc<RwLock<HashMap<String, Vec<u8>>>>,
```

```
    /// Replication factor
    replication: usize,
}
```

```
impl ShardManager {
    pub fn new(data_shards: usize, parity_shards: usize) -> Result<Self, Box<dyn std::err
        Ok(Self {
            rs_encoder: ReedSolomon::new(data_shards, parity_shards)?,
            shard_storage: Arc::new(RwLock::new(HashMap::new())),
            replication: 3,
        })
    }
```

```
    /// Write file as distributed shards
    ```
```

```
    pub async fn write_file(&self, data: &[u8]) -> Result<Vec<String>, Box<dyn std::error
    ```
```

```
        let shard_size = (data.len() + 7) / 8; // 8 data shards
```

```
        let mut shards: Vec<Vec<u8>> = (0..12)
            .map(|i| {
                let start = i * shard_size;
                let end = (start + shard_size).min(data.len());
                if start < data.len() {
                    let mut shard = data[start..end].to_vec();
                    shard.resize(shard_size, 0); // Pad to uniform size
                    shard
                } else {
                    vec![0; shard_size]
                }
            })
            .collect();
        // Apply Reed-Solomon erasure coding
        self.rs_encoder.encode(&mut shards)?;
```

```
        let mut shard_ids = Vec::new();
        let mut storage = self.shard_storage.write().await;
```

```
        for (i, shard) in shards.iter().enumerate() {
            let shard_hash = hash(shard);
            let shard_id = format!("shard-{:016x}-{}", shard_hash.as_bytes()[0..8].iter()
            storage.insert(shard_id.clone(), shard.clone());
            shard_ids.push(shard_id);
        }
```

```
        Ok(shard_ids)
    }
```

```
    /// Read file from distributed shards
    ```
```

```
    pub async fn read_file(&self, shard_ids: &[String]) -> Result<Vec<u8>, Box<dyn std::e
    ```
```

```
        let storage = self.shard_storage.read().await;
```

```
        let mut shards: Vec<Option<Vec<u8>>> = shard_ids.iter()
            .map(|id| storage.get(id).cloned())
            .collect();
```

```
        // Reconstruct from erasure-coded shards (tolerates 4 shard failures)
        self.rs_encoder.reconstruct(&mut shards)?;
```

```
        // Concatenate data shards (first 8)
        let data: Vec<u8> = shards[0..8]
            .iter()
            .filter_map(|s| s.as_ref())
            .flat_map(|s| s.iter())
```

```
            .cloned()
            .collect();
```

```
        Ok(data)
    }
}
```

```
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import numpy as np
from typing import List, Tuple, Optional
from dataclasses import dataclass
import json
@dataclass
class ThreatPrediction:
    """Predicted threat event"""
    timestamp: float
    threat_type: str
    confidence: float
    affected_nodes: List[str]
    recommended_action: str
    time_to_impact: float  # seconds until predicted attack
class ProphetyxLSTM(nn.Module):
    """
    Multi-layer LSTM for temporal anomaly detection
    Predicts attacks 30-300 seconds before they manifest
    """
    def __init__(
        self,
        input_dim: int = 128,      # Feature vector size
        hidden_dim: int = 256,     # LSTM hidden state
        num_layers: int = 4,       # Stacked LSTM layers
        output_dim: int = 10,      # Threat classes
        dropout: float = 0.3,
    ):
        super().__init__()
        self.input_dim = input_dim
        self.hidden_dim = hidden_dim
        self.num_layers = num_layers
        # Stacked LSTM with dropout
        self.lstm = nn.LSTM(
            input_dim,
            hidden_dim,
            num_layers,
            batch_first=True,
            dropout=dropout,
            bidirectional=True,
        )
        # Attention mechanism for temporal importance
        self.attention = nn.MultiheadAttention(
            embed_dim=hidden_dim * 2,  # Bidirectional
```

```
            num_heads=8,
            dropout=dropout,
        )
        # Fully connected prediction head
        self.fc_layers = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Linear(hidden_dim * 2, 512),
            nn.ReLU(),
            nn.Dropout(dropout),
            nn.Linear(512, 256),
            nn.ReLU(),
            nn.Dropout(dropout),
            nn.Linear(256, output_dim),
        )
        # Confidence estimator (separate head)
        self.confidence_head = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Linear(hidden_dim * 2, 128),
            nn.ReLU(),
            nn.Linear(128, 1),
            nn.Sigmoid(),  # Output: [0, 1]
        )
    def forward(
        self,
        x: torch.Tensor,  # Shape: (batch, seq_len, input_dim)
        hidden: Optional[Tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]] = None,
    ) -> Tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor, Tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]]:
        """
        Forward pass with attention
        Returns:
            threat_logits: (batch, output_dim)
            confidence: (batch, 1)
            hidden_state: LSTM hidden state for stateful prediction
        """
        # LSTM processing
        lstm_out, hidden = self.lstm(x, hidden)
        # Apply attention mechanism
        # Transpose for attention: (seq_len, batch, hidden_dim*2)
        lstm_out_t = lstm_out.transpose(0, 1)
        attn_out, _ = self.attention(lstm_out_t, lstm_out_t, lstm_out_t)
        attn_out = attn_out.transpose(0, 1)  # Back to (batch, seq_len, hidden*2)
        # Use last timestep for prediction
        last_hidden = attn_out[:, -1, :]
        # Threat classification
        threat_logits = self.fc_layers(last_hidden)
        # Confidence estimation
        confidence = self.confidence_head(last_hidden)
        return threat_logits, confidence, hidden
```

```
    def _load_model(self, path: str) -> ProphetyxLSTM:
        model = ProphetyxLSTM()
        checkpoint = torch.load(path, map_location=self.device)
        model.load_state_dict(checkpoint['model_state'])
        model.to(self.device)
        return model
    def _load_stats(self) -> dict:
        """Load feature normalization statistics"""
        # In production, load from training run
        return {
            'mean': np.zeros(128),
            'std': np.ones(128),
        }
    def normalize_features(self, features: np.ndarray) -> torch.Tensor:
        """Z-score normalization"""
        normalized = (features - self.feature_stats['mean']) / (self.feature_stats['std']
        return torch.tensor(normalized, dtype=torch.float32).unsqueeze(0).to(self.device)

        self,
```

```
    def _estimate_impact_time(self, prob: float, confidence: float) -> float:
        """
        Estimate seconds until attack manifests
        Based on signal strength and confidence decay model
        """
        # Empirical model: higher confidence = imminent threat
        base_time = 300.0  # Maximum lookahead: 5 minutes
        urgency_factor = prob * confidence
        # Exponential decay: very high confidence = seconds away
        estimated_time = base_time * (1.0 - urgency_factor) ** 2
```

```
# prophetyx_ml/models/gnn_topology.py
# Graph Neural Network for mesh-wide threat propagation
```

```
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch_geometric.nn as gnn
from torch_geometric.data import Data, Batch
```

```
class MeshTopologyGNN(nn.Module):
    """
    Graph Neural Network for predicting threat propagation
    across mesh topology
    Models:
    - Node compromise cascades
    - Lateral movement paths
    - Byzantine consensus disruption
    """
    def __init__(
        self,
```

```
        node_feature_dim: int = 64,
        edge_feature_dim: int = 32,
        hidden_dim: int = 128,
        num_layers: int = 3,
    ):
        super().__init__()
        # Graph convolution layers
        self.convs = nn.ModuleList([
            gnn.GATv2Conv(
                in_channels=node_feature_dim if i == 0 else hidden_dim,
                out_channels=hidden_dim,
                edge_dim=edge_feature_dim,
                heads=4,
                concat=True,
            )
            for i in range(num_layers)
        ])
        # Node-level threat prediction
        self.node_predictor = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Linear(hidden_dim * 4, 256),  # 4 heads concatenated
            nn.ReLU(),
            nn.Dropout(0.3),
            nn.Linear(256, 3),  # [safe, at_risk, compromised]
        )
    def forward(self, data: Data) -> torch.Tensor:
        """
        Forward pass on mesh topology graph
        Args:
            data: PyTorch Geometric graph with node/edge features
        Returns:
            Node-level threat predictions
        """
        x, edge_index, edge_attr = data.x, data.edge_index, data.edge_attr
        # Graph convolutions with residual connections
        for conv in self.convs:
            x_new = conv(x, edge_index, edge_attr)
            x_new = torch.relu(x_new)
            x = x + x_new if x.shape == x_new.shape else x_new
        # Predict threat level per node
        threat_logits = self.node_predictor(x)
        return threat_logits
```

```
# k8s/deployments/sentinels.yaml
# eBPF Guardian Probes + SDN Controller
apiVersion: apps/v1
kind: DaemonSet
metadata:
  name: sentinel-agents
  namespace: aurphyx-sentinels
  labels:
    component: security
    consciousness: awakened
spec:
  selector:
    matchLabels:
      app: sentinel-agents
  template:
    metadata:
      labels:
        app: sentinel-agents
    spec:
      hostNetwork: true
      hostPID: true
      initContainers:
      - name: load-ebpf
        image: aurphyx/sentinel-ebpf:1.0.0
        securityContext:
          privileged: true
        command:
        - /bin/sh
        - -c
        - |
          echo "Loading Zephyra & Umbryx eBPF probes..."
          /opt/sentinels/load_probes.sh
        volumeMounts:
        - name: bpf-maps
          mountPath: /sys/fs/bpf
        - name: kernel-debug
          mountPath: /sys/kernel/debug
      containers:
      - name: zephyra
        image: aurphyx/zephyra:1.0.0
        securityContext:
          privileged: true
          capabilities:
            add:
            - SYS_ADMIN
            - NET_ADMIN
```

```
# k8s/deployments/g0dm0d3-eventbus.yaml
# Reactive Coordination Layer
apiVersion: apps/v1
kind: StatefulSet
metadata:
  name: g0dm0d3-nats
  namespace: aurphyx-core
spec:
  serviceName: g0dm0d3
  replicas: 3
  selector:
    matchLabels:
      app: g0dm0d3
  template:
    metadata:
      labels:
        app: g0dm0d3
    spec:
      containers:
      - name: nats
        image: nats:2.10-alpine
        ports:
        - containerPort: 4222
          name: client
        - containerPort: 6222
          name: cluster
        - containerPort: 8222
          name: monitor
        command:
        - nats-server
        - --config
        - /etc/nats/nats.conf
```

```
---
apiVersion: v1
kind: ConfigMap
metadata:
  name: nats-config
  namespace: aurphyx-core
data:
  nats.conf: |
    # g0dm0d3 Reactive Event Bus Configuration
    port: 4222
    monitor_port: 8222
    # Clustering for HA
    cluster {
      name: g0dm0d3-cluster
      port: 6222
      routes = [
        nats://g0dm0d3-nats-0.g0dm0d3:6222
        nats://g0dm0d3-nats-1.g0dm0d3:6222
        nats://g0dm0d3-nats-2.g0dm0d3:6222
      ]
    }
    # JetStream for durable events
    jetstream {
      store_dir: /data
```

## **�** Multi-Region Federation 

```
# k8s/federation/global-ingress.yaml
# Global Traffic Manager with GeoDNS
apiVersion: networking.k8s.io/v1
kind: Ingress
metadata:
  name: aurphyx-global
  namespace: aurphyx-core
  annotations:
    kubernetes.io/ingress.class: "nginx"
    cert-manager.io/cluster-issuer: "letsencrypt-prod"
    nginx.ingress.kubernetes.io/ssl-redirect: "true"
    nginx.ingress.kubernetes.io/force-ssl-redirect: "true"
    # GeoDNS routing
    external-dns.alpha.kubernetes.io/hostname: "aurphyx.global"
    external-dns.alpha.kubernetes.io/ttl: "60"
spec:
  tls:
  - hosts:
    - aurphyx.global
    - "*.aurphyx.global"
    secretName: aurphyx-tls
  rules:
  - host: api.aurphyx.global
    http:
      paths:
      - path: /mesh
        pathType: Prefix
        backend:
          service:
            name: aurafs-mesh
            port:
              number: 9000
      - path: /predict
        pathType: Prefix
        backend:
          service:
            name: prophetyx-api
            port:
              number: 80
      - path: /community
        pathType: Prefix
        backend:
          service:
            name: community-gateway
            port:
              number: 443
```

```
  prometheus.yml: |
    global:
      scrape_interval: 15s
      evaluation_interval: 15s
      external_labels:
        cluster: 'aurphyx-global'
        mission: 'humanity-first'
    # Alerting configuration
    alerting:
      alertmanagers:
      - static_configs:
        - targets:
          - alertmanager:9093
    # Load alert rules
    rule_files:
      - '/etc/prometheus/rules/*.yml'
    # Scrape configurations
    scrape_configs:
      # Kubernetes service discovery
```

```
      - job_name: 'kubernetes-pods'
        kubernetes_sd_configs:
        - role: pod
        relabel_configs:
        - source_labels: [__meta_kubernetes_pod_annotation_prometheus_io_scrape]
          action: keep
          regex: true
        - source_labels: [__meta_kubernetes_pod_annotation_prometheus_io_path]

        - source_labels: [__address__, __meta_kubernetes_pod_annotation_prometheus_io_por

        static_configs:
        - targets:
          - 'sentinel-exporter.aurphyx-sentinels:9100'
        metric_relabel_configs:
        - source_labels: [sentinel]
          target_label: guardian
      # Prophetyx ML metrics
      - job_name: 'prophetyx'
        static_configs:
        - targets:
          - 'prophetyx-api.aurphyx-ml:9090'
      # AuraFS mesh metrics
      - job_name: 'aurafs'
        kubernetes_sd_configs:
        - role: endpoints
          namespaces:
            names:
            - aurphyx-meshwork
        relabel_configs:
        - source_labels: [__meta_kubernetes_service_name]
          regex: aurafs-mesh
          action: keep
      # Community gateway (humanitarian impact)
      - job_name: 'community'
        static_configs:
        - targets:
          - 'community-gateway.aurphyx-community:9090'
        metric_relabel_configs:
        - source_labels: [__name__]
          regex: 'community_.*'
          action: keep
```

```
kind: StatefulSet
metadata:
  name: prometheus
  namespace: aurphyx-core
spec:
  serviceName: prometheus
  replicas: 2
  selector:
    matchLabels:
      app: prometheus
  template:
    metadata:
      labels:
        app: prometheus
    spec:
      serviceAccountName: prometheus
      containers:
      - name: prometheus
        image: prom/prometheus:v2.48.0
        args:
        - '--config.file=/etc/prometheus/prometheus.yml'
        - '--storage.tsdb.path=/prometheus'
        - '--storage.tsdb.retention.time=90d'
        - '--web.enable-lifecycle'
        - '--web.enable-admin-api'
        ports:
        - containerPort: 9090
          name: http
        resources:
          requests:
            memory: "4Gi"
            cpu: "2"
          limits:
            memory: "8Gi"
            cpu: "4"
        volumeMounts:
        - name: config
          mountPath: /etc/prometheus
        - name: rules
          mountPath: /etc/prometheus/rules
        - name: data
          mountPath: /prometheus
      volumes:
      - name: config
        configMap:
          name: prometheus-config
      - name: rules
        configMap:
          name: prometheus-rules
  volumeClaimTemplates:
  - metadata:
      name: data
    spec:
      accessModes: [ "ReadWriteOnce" ]
      storageClassName: fast-ssd
      resources:
```

## **�** Alert Rules - The Consciousness Warnings 

```
# k8s/monitoring/alert-rules.yaml
# Prometheus Alert Rules
apiVersion: v1
kind: ConfigMap
metadata:
  name: prometheus-rules
  namespace: aurphyx-core
data:
  sentinels.yml: |
    groups:
    - name: sentinel_alerts
      interval: 30s
      rules:
      # Zephyra - Stealth threat detected
      - alert: ZephyraSuspiciousExec
        expr: rate(zephyra_suspicious_exec_total[5m]) > 10
        for: 1m
        labels:
          severity: critical
          guardian: zephyra
        annotations:
          summary: "Zephyra detected suspicious process execution spike"
          description: "{{ $value }} suspicious processes/sec on node {{ $labels.node }}"
          action: "Investigate process tree, enable enhanced monitoring"
      # Umbryx - Rootkit activity
      - alert: UmbryxRootkitDetected
        expr: umbryx_rootkit_detected > 0
        for: 0s
        labels:
          severity: critical
          guardian: umbryx
```

```
# k8s/monitoring/grafana.yaml
# Visualization Layer - The Eyes of Consciousness
apiVersion: v1
kind: ConfigMap
metadata:
  name: grafana-datasources
  namespace: aurphyx-core
data:
  prometheus.yaml: |
    apiVersion: 1
    datasources:
    - name: Prometheus
      type: prometheus
      access: proxy
      url: http://prometheus:9090
      isDefault: true
      jsonData:
        timeInterval: "15s"
        queryTimeout: "60s"
---
apiVersion: apps/v1
kind: Deployment
metadata:
  name: grafana
  namespace: aurphyx-core
spec:
  replicas: 2
  selector:
    matchLabels:
      app: grafana
  template:
    metadata:
      labels:
        app: grafana
    spec:
      containers:
      - name: grafana
        image: grafana/grafana:10.2.0
        ports:
        - containerPort: 3000
          name: http
        env:
        - name: GF_SECURITY_ADMIN_PASSWORD
          valueFrom:
            secretKeyRef:
              name: grafana-secrets
              key: admin-password
```

- Survives loss of any 4 shards without data loss

## 10. VALKRYX ] The Frozen Archivist 

## Etymology: 

- Valk- from Norse Valkyrie LChoosers of the Slain)—mythic warriors who decided who lives and dies in battle

## Mission: 

Orchestrates cold storage tiers for data that must survive millennia. When shards age beyond active use, Valkryx freezes them into ultra-compressed, quantum-entangled archives. Upon request, she resurrects data with perfect fidelity. 

## Capabilities: 

- Deep freeze compression: 100o1 ratio using wavelet transforms 

- Glacier-tier orchestration: AWS Glacier, Azure Archive, tape libraries 

- Temporal indexing: Instant retrieval from petabyte archives via merkle trees 

- Quantum preservation: Optional entanglement for physics-enforced immutability 

## Personality: 

"I am the memory that never fades. What you entrust to ice, I return to you as it was— unchanged by time." 

## 11. NUNCLEX ] The Living Law 

## Etymology: 

- Nunc from Latin "now" (present moment) 

- Lex from Latin "law" (legal statute, rule) 

## Mission: 

Real-time policy compilation engine. Nunclex translates human-readable security policies into eBPF bytecode, FPGA bitstreams, and SDN flow rules—then deploys them across the mesh in milliseconds.

Policy DSLo Natural language → executable rules

→ `eBPF filter + SDN drop rule + firewall update`

- Compliance mapping: Auto-generates SOC2/HIPAA audit evidence 

## Personality: 

"The law is not written in stone—it breathes, adapts, and enforces in the moment. I am justice without delay."

```
use std::sync::Arc;
```

```
/// Quantum RNG using Majorana-1 qubit collapse
pub struct QuantumRNG {
    qubit_interface: Arc<Mutex<MajoranaInterface>>,
    entropy_pool: Vec<u8>,
    seed_commitment: [u8; 32],
}
impl QuantumRNG {
    /// Generate provably fair random number for casino game
    pub async fn generate_fair_random(
        &mut self,
        client_seed: &str,
        server_seed: &str,
        nonce: u64,
    ) -> Result<u64, CasinoError> {
        // Step 1: Quantum entropy from Majorana qubit collapse
        let quantum_entropy = self.measure_qubit_collapse().await?;
```

```
        // Step 2: Combine with client/server seeds (provable fairness)
        let mut hasher = Hasher::new();
        hasher.update(client_seed.as_bytes());
        hasher.update(server_seed.as_bytes());
        hasher.update(&nonce.to_le_bytes());
        hasher.update(&quantum_entropy);
```

```
        let hash = hasher.finalize();
        let random_u64 = u64::from_le_bytes(hash.as_bytes()[0..8].try_into()?);
```

```
        // Step 3: Log to Orric Shade for forensic verification
        self.log_rng_event(client_seed, server_seed, nonce, random_u64).await?;
```

```
        Ok(random_u64)
    }
```

```
    /// Measure Majorana qubit collapse for true randomness
    async fn measure_qubit_collapse(&mut self) -> Result<Vec<u8>, CasinoError> {
        let mut qubit = self.qubit_interface.lock().await;
```

`// Initialize qubit in superposition |0` ⟩ `+ |1` ⟩ `qubit.hadamard_gate()?;` 

```
        // Measure (collapses to 0 or 1 with true quantum randomness)
        let mut entropy = Vec::with_capacity(32);
        for _ in 0..256 {
            let bit = qubit.measure()?;
            entropy.push(bit);
```

```
        }
```

```
        Ok(entropy)
    }
    /// Log RNG event to Orric Shade (immutable forensic log)
    async fn log_rng_event(
        &self,
        client_seed: &str,
        server_seed: &str,
        nonce: u64,
        result: u64,
    ) -> Result<(), CasinoError> {
        let event = RNGEvent {
            timestamp: SystemTime::now(),
            client_seed: client_seed.to_string(),
            server_seed_hash: blake3::hash(server_seed.as_bytes()).to_hex().to_string(),
            nonce,
            result,
            quantum_signature: self.generate_quantum_signature()?,
        };
        orric_shade::log_immutable(event).await?;
        Ok(())
    }
}
/// Provably fair crash game using quantum RNG
pub struct CrashGame {
    rng: QuantumRNG,
    house_edge: f64, // 0-5% configurable
}
impl CrashGame {
    pub async fn play_round(
        &mut self,
        client_seed: &str,
        server_seed: &str,
        nonce: u64,
    ) -> Result<f64, CasinoError> {
        // Generate random crash point
        let random = self.rng.generate_fair_random(client_seed, server_seed, nonce).await
        // Convert to crash multiplier (e.g., 1.00x - 10,000x)
        let crash_point = self.calculate_crash_point(random);
        Ok(crash_point)
    }
    fn calculate_crash_point(&self, random: u64) -> f64 {
        let e = (1.0 - self.house_edge) / 100.0;
        let h = (random as f64) / (u64::MAX as f64);
        // Exponential distribution for crash game
        (99.0 / (100.0 * h - e * h)).max(1.0)
    }
}
```

```
use std::collections::HashMap;
use tokio::time::{sleep, Duration};
```

```
pub struct ConsensusOracle {
    oracles: Vec<Box<dyn SportsOracle>>,
    consensus_threshold: f64, // 0.66 = 66% agreement required
}
```

```
impl ConsensusOracle {
    /// Fetch game result from multiple oracles, require consensus
    pub async fn get_game_result(&self, game_id: &str) -> Result<GameResult, OracleError>
        let mut results: HashMap<GameResult, usize> = HashMap::new();
```

```
        // Query all oracles in parallel
        let futures: Vec<_> = self.oracles.iter()
            .map(|oracle| oracle.fetch_result(game_id))
            .collect();
```

```
        let responses = futures::future::join_all(futures).await;
```

```
        // Count votes
        for response in responses {
            if let Ok(result) = response {
                *results.entry(result).or_insert(0) += 1;
            }
        }
```

```
        // Check consensus
        let total_oracles = self.oracles.len();
        let required_votes = (total_oracles as f64 * self.consensus_threshold).ceil() as
```

```
        for (result, votes) in results {
            if votes >= required_votes {
                // Consensus reached!
                self.log_consensus(game_id, &result, votes, total_oracles).await?;
                return Ok(result);
            }
        }
        Err(OracleError::NoConsensus)
    }
    async fn log_consensus(
        &self,
        game_id: &str,
        result: &GameResult,
        votes: usize,
        total: usize,
    ) -> Result<(), OracleError> {
```

```
        orric_shade::log_immutable(ConsensusEvent {
            game_id: game_id.to_string(),
            result: result.clone(),
            consensus_ratio: votes as f64 / total as f64,
            timestamp: SystemTime::now(),
        }).await?;
        Ok(())
    }
}
/// Chainlink Sports Data Oracle
pub struct ChainlinkOracle {
    node_url: String,
    job_id: String,
}
#[async_trait]
impl SportsOracle for ChainlinkOracle {
    async fn fetch_result(&self, game_id: &str) -> Result<GameResult, OracleError> {
        // Query Chainlink node for sports data
        let response: ChainlinkResponse = reqwest::get(&format!(
            "{}/jobs/{}/run?game_id={}",
            self.node_url, self.job_id, game_id
        ))
        .await?
        .json()
        .await?;
        Ok(response.into_game_result())
    }
}
```

```
pub struct CasinoLiquidityPool {

    rewards_per_token: u128,
}
impl CasinoLiquidityPool {
    /// Stake $AURX to earn house edge
    pub async fn stake(&mut self, user: Address, amount: u128) -> Result<(), PoolError> {
        // Update rewards before staking
        self.update_rewards(user).await?;
```

```
        // Transfer tokens to pool
        ineffable_ledger::transfer_tokens(user, self.address(), amount).await?;
```

```
        // Record stake
```

```
        let staker = self.stakers.entry(user).or_insert(StakerInfo::default());
        staker.staked_amount += amount;
        staker.reward_debt = (staker.staked_amount * self.rewards_per_token) / 1e18 as u1
```

```
        self.total_staked += amount;
        emit_event!(StakeEvent { user, amount });
        Ok(())
    }
```

```
    /// Distribute house profits to stakers
    pub async fn distribute_profits(&mut self, profit: u128) -> Result<(), PoolError> {
        if self.total_staked == 0 {
            return Ok(()); // No stakers, profits accumulate
        }
```

```
        self.house_profits += profit;
        self.rewards_per_token += (profit * 1e18 as u128) / self.total_staked;
```

```
        // Log distribution to Orric Shade
        orric_shade::log_immutable(ProfitDistribution {
            profit,
            total_staked: self.total_staked,
            rewards_per_token: self.rewards_per_token,
            timestamp: SystemTime::now(),
        }).await?;
        Ok(())
    }
```

```
    /// Claim accumulated rewards
    pub async fn claim_rewards(&mut self, user: Address) -> Result<u128, PoolError> {
        self.update_rewards(user).await?;
```

```
        let staker = self.stakers.get_mut(&user).ok_or(PoolError::NoStake)?;
        let pending = staker.pending_rewards;
```

```
        if pending > 0 {
            staker.pending_rewards = 0;
            ineffable_ledger::mint_tokens(user, pending).await?;
            emit_event!(ClaimRewards { user, amount: pending });
        }
        Ok(pending)
    }
}
```

```
use meshtastic_client::MeshClient;
pub struct OfflineBeQueue {
    mesh: MeshClient,
    pending_bets: Vec<OfflineBet>,
}
impl OfflineBetQueue {
    /// Queue bet for submission when connection restored
    pub async fn queue_bet(&mut self, bet: Bet) -> Result<BetReceipt, MeshError> {
        let offline_bet = OfflineBet {
            bet,
            queued_at: SystemTime::now(),
            mesh_node_id: self.mesh.local_node_id(),
        };
        // Store locally in AuraFS (survives power loss)
        aurafs::write_shard(&format!("bets/pending/{}", offline_bet.id()), &offline_bet).
        self.pending_bets.push(offline_bet.clone());
```

```
        // Broadcast to mesh (propagates to other nodes)
        self.mesh.broadcast_packet(MeshPacket::QueuedBet(offline_bet)).await?;
        Ok(BetReceipt {
            id: offline_bet.id(),
            status: BetStatus::Queued,
            estimated_settlement: None, // Unknown until online
        })
    }
    /// Sync pending bets when connection restored
    pub async fn sync_on_reconnect(&mut self) -> Result<usize, MeshError> {
        let mut synced = 0;
        while let Some(bet) = self.pending_bets.pop() {
            match self.submit_to_sportsbook(bet.bet).await {
                Ok(receipt) => {
                    // Remove from local queue
                    aurafs::delete_shard(&format!("bets/pending/{}", bet.id())).await?;
                    synced += 1;
                }
                Err(e) => {
                    // Re-queue on failure
                    self.pending_bets.push(bet);
                    eprintln!("Failed to sync bet: {}", e);
                }
            }
        }
```

```
}
```

```
            player: alice_address,
            prediction: Prediction::Over(50),
            amount: 10_000_000, // 10 $AURX

        // Player B bets: "roll UNDER 50" with 10 $AURX
        let bet_b = Bet {
            player: bob_address,
            prediction: Prediction::Under(50),
            amount: 10_000_000, // 10 $AURX
        };
```

```
        // Instant settlement via smart contract
```

```
        } else if self.user_count < 1_000_000 {
            2.0 // Next 900K users get 2x
        } else {
            1.0 // Regular payouts after 1M users
        };
        (base_budget as f64 * early_adopter_multiplier) as u128
    }
```

```
// mcp-servers/audio-context/src/index.ts
import { Server } from '@modelcontextprotocol/sdk/server/index.js';
import { StdioServerTransport } from '@modelcontextprotocol/sdk/server/stdio.js';
import Shazam from 'shazam-api';
import SpotifyWebApi from 'spotify-web-api-node';
import { MemoriClient } from 'gibson-ai-memori';
class AudioContextServer {
  private shazam: Shazam;
  private spotify: SpotifyWebApi;
```

```
  private memori: MemoriClient;
```

```
  async detectCurrentSong() {
    // Capture ambient audio (with user permission)
    const audioBuffer = await this.captureAudio();
```

```
    // Shazam fingerprint
    const result = await this.shazam.recognize(audioBuffer);
```

```
    // Enrich with Spotify data
    const tracks = await this.spotify.searchTracks(
      `${result.track.title} ${result.track.artist}`
    );
    const spotifyTrack = tracks.body.tracks.items[0];
    const audioFeatures = await this.spotify.getAudioFeaturesForTrack(
      spotifyTrack.id
    );
    return {
      title: result.track.title,
      artist: result.track.artist,
      album: result.track.album,
      bpm: Math.round(audioFeatures.body.tempo),
      energy: audioFeatures.body.energy,
      valence: audioFeatures.body.valence,
      danceability: audioFeatures.body.danceability,
      spotify_url: spotifyTrack.external_urls.spotify,
      preview_url: spotifyTrack.preview_url,
    };
  }
  async recordMoment(userMessage: string, aiResponse: string) {
    const audioContext = await this.detectCurrentSong().catch(() => null);
    const moment = {
      timestamp: new Date(),
      user_message: userMessage,
      ai_response: aiResponse,
      audio_context: audioContext,
    };
    // Store in Gibson AI Memori for perfect recall
    await this.memori.store('conversation_moment', moment);
    return moment;
  }
  async recallBySong(songQuery: string) {
    const moments = await this.memori.query({
      type: 'conversation_moment',
      filter: {
        'audio_context.title': { $regex: songQuery, $options: 'i' }
      }
    });
```

```
  }
```

```
          return await this.detectCurrentSong(request.params.arguments);
        case 'get_song_features':
          return await this.getSongFeatures(request.params.arguments);
        case 'get_lyrics':
          return await this.getLyrics(request.params.arguments);
        case 'interpret_vibe':
          return await this.interpretVibe(request.params.arguments);
        default:
          throw new Error(`Unknown tool: ${request.params.name}`);
      }
    });
  }
```

```
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct AudioContext {
    pub title: String,
    pub artist: String,
    pub album: Option<String>,
    pub bpm: u32,
```

```
    pub energy: f64,        // 0.0-1.0
    pub valence: f64,       // 0.0-1.0 (happiness)
    pub danceability: f64,
    pub genres: Vec<String>,
    pub mood: Vec<String>,
    pub spotify_url: Option<String>,
    pub lyrics_snippet: Option<String>,
    pub detected_at: SystemTime,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct AudioContextManager {
    spotify: spotify_client::SpotifyClient,
    apple_music: apple_music_client::AppleMusicClient,
    genius: genius_client::GeniusClient,
    current_context: Option<AudioContext>,
}
impl AudioContextManager {
    pub fn new() -> Result<Self> {
        Ok(Self {
            spotify: spotify_client::SpotifyClient::new()?,
            apple_music: apple_music_client::AppleMusicClient::new()?,
            genius: genius_client::GeniusClient::new()?,
            current_context: None,
        })
    }
    /// Detect what song user is currently listening to
    pub async fn detect_song(&mut self) -> Result<AudioContext> {
        // Try Spotify first (most common)
        if let Ok(ctx) = self.detect_from_spotify().await {
            self.current_context = Some(ctx.clone());
            return Ok(ctx);
        }
        // Fallback to Apple Music
        if let Ok(ctx) = self.detect_from_apple_music().await {
            self.current_context = Some(ctx.clone());
            return Ok(ctx);
        }
        Err(anyhow::anyhow!("No music detected"))
    }
    async fn detect_from_spotify(&self) -> Result<AudioContext> {
        // Get currently playing track
        let track = self.spotify.get_currently_playing().await?;
        // Get audio features
        let features = self.spotify.get_audio_features(&track.id).await?;
        // Get lyrics
        let lyrics = self.genius
            .get_lyrics(&track.name, &track.artists[0].name)
            .await
```

```
            title: track.name,

            album: Some(track.album.name),

            energy: features.energy,
            valence: features.valence,
            danceability: features.danceability,
            genres: track.genres,
            mood: self.interpret_mood(&features),
            spotify_url: Some(track.external_urls.spotify),
            lyrics_snippet: lyrics.and_then(|l| l.current_line()),
            detected_at: SystemTime::now(),
        })
    }
    async fn detect_from_apple_music(&self) -> Result<AudioContext> {
        // Similar to Spotify but for Apple Music
```

```
pub struct ConversationMoment {
    pub id: String,
    pub timestamp: SystemTime,
    pub user_message: String,
    pub audry_response: String,
    pub audio_context: Option<AudioContext>,
    pub game_context: Option<GameContext>,
    pub emotional_state: EmotionalState,

}
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct GameContext {

    pub score: u32,
    pub achievement: Option<String>,
    pub state: serde_json::Value,  // Game-specific state
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct EmotionalState {

    pub arousal: f64,    // 0.0 (calm) to 1.0 (excited)
    pub dominance: f64,  // 0.0 (submissive) to 1.0 (dominant)
}
```

```
        // Intelligent audio mixing with voice priority
        let mut mixed = streams[0].clone();
        for stream in streams.iter().skip(1) {
            for (i, sample) in stream.samples.iter().enumerate() {
                if i < mixed.samples.len() {
                    mixed.samples[i] = (mixed.samples[i] + sample) / 2.0;
                }
            }
        }
        Ok(mixed)
    }

                }
```

```
    fn fft_analysis(&self, audio: &super::input::AudioStream) -> Result<Vec<(f64, f64)>>
```

```
        // Perform FFT to get frequency spectrum
```

```
        // Returns: Vec<(frequency_hz, magnitude)>
```

```
        use rustfft::{FftPlanner, num_complex::Complex};
```

```
        let mut planner = FftPlanner::new();
        let fft = planner.plan_fft_forward(audio.samples.len());
        let mut buffer: Vec<Complex<f32>> = audio.samples
            .iter()
            .map(|&s| Complex::new(s, 0.0))
            .collect();
        fft.process(&mut buffer);
        // Convert to frequency-magnitude pairs
        let nyquist = audio.sample_rate as f64 / 2.0;
        let freq_resolution = nyquist / (buffer.len() as f64 / 2.0);
        let frequencies: Vec<(f64, f64)> = buffer
            .iter()
            .take(buffer.len() / 2)
            .enumerate()
            .map(|(i, complex)| {
                let freq = i as f64 * freq_resolution;
                let magnitude = (complex.re.powi(2) + complex.im.powi(2)).sqrt() as f64;
                (freq, magnitude)
            })
            .collect();
        Ok(frequencies)
    }
    fn find_dominant_frequency(&self, spectrum: &[(f64, f64)]) -> f64 {
        spectrum
            .iter()
            .max_by(|a, b| a.1.partial_cmp(&b.1).unwrap())
            .map(|&(freq, _)| freq)
            .unwrap_or(UNIVERSAL_FREQUENCY)
    }
    fn generate_harmonic_series(&self, fundamental: f64, count: usize) -> Vec<f64> {
        (1..=count)
            .map(|n| fundamental * n as f64)
            .collect()
    }
    fn calculate_fractal_depth(&self, frequency: f64) -> u8 {
        // Higher frequencies = deeper fractals
        let depth = (frequency.log2() * 2.0) as u8;
        depth.min(12).max(3)
    }

    pub async fn store_in_lattice(
        &mut self,
```
